+# Changelog — Peek Explorer
+
+All notable changes to **Peek Explorer** (`e:\Vector Field Theory\peek_explorer`) are documented in this file.
+
+---
+
+## [v0.8.8] — 2026-10-25
+### Added
+- **Native Address Bar Parity & Ergonomic Accessibility**:
+  - **Location Icon (`#addrIcon`)**: Added dynamic icon (`🏠` Home, `🧬` Gluon, `💾` Drive, `🌐` UNC Network Share, `📁` Folder) to the left of the address bar. Clicking this icon immediately converts breadcrumbs into the editable URL input with the entire path selected for 1-click copying.
+  - **Instant Mousedown Activation**: Clicking anywhere in the address bar (empty space outside breadcrumb links) instantly enters edit mode on `mousedown`, eliminating the delay and click-misses of waiting for mouseup.
+  - **Non-Clearing Address Text**: `startAddressEdit()` now preserves and displays the active URI (including `peek://home` and `peek://gluon`) rather than clearing the input, making internal schemes copyable and transparent.
+  - **Selection Retention Safeguard (`justStartedAddrEdit`)**: Enforced atomic text selection (`$('address').select()`) using `requestAnimationFrame` + `mouseup` capture so that releasing the mouse never deselects the text.
+  - **Address History Chevron Dropdown (`#addrHistBtn` `▾`)**: Dedicated dropdown chevron at the right edge of the address bar displaying recent locations, Home, Semantic Gluons, and a 1-click "Copy current path" option.
+  - **Address Bar Context Menu**: Right-clicking the address bar now surfaces native Windows Explorer actions: "Edit address", "Copy address", "Copy address as text", and "Refresh (F5)".
+  - **Styling Refinements**: Added Windows 11 subtle hover borders, focus ring, and clean flex layout.
+  - **Automated Smoke Tests**: Added assertions verifying `#addrIcon`, `#addrHistBtn`, and 1-click address bar text selection (30/30 tests pass).
+
+---
+
+## [v0.8.7] — 2026-10-25
+### Added
+- **Semantic Gluon DNA Engine (`gluon_engine.js`)**:
+  - Full client-side pure-JS port of `unified_gluon_dna_engine.py` (v5.0 Generation 4 & 5 Universal Synthesis).
+  - **QCD $SU(3)$ Color Gauge Physics**:
+    - Evaluates Red ($cr$: Thesis / Will $+W$), Green ($cg$: Operational Context / Action $+A$), and Blue ($cb$: Deductive Constraint / Invariant Proof $-N, -X$).
+    - Computes **White Hadron Singlet Baryon** balance ($cr \ge 0.12, cg \ge 0.12, cb \ge 0.12, \Delta \le 0.35$) to verify epistemologically balanced reasoning vs ideological skew.
+  - **Cornell Confinement Potential & String Snapping**:
+    - Calculates $V(r) = -\frac{\alpha_s}{r+\epsilon} + \sigma r$ with QCD strong coupling $\alpha_s = 0.35$ and string tension $\sigma = 0.85$.
+    - Detects section-by-section string snapping at critical radius $r_c = 1.529$, flagging non-sequitur leaps across paragraphs.
+  - **The 7 Planes of Reality across the 42-Structure**:
+    - Classifies documents and sections into $Q_1$ (Metaphysical Driver / Who), $Q_2$ (Possible / What), $Q_3$ (Physical / Where), $Q_4$ (Lyrical / Why), $Q_5$ (Logical / How), $Q_6$ (Historical / Cause), and $Q_7$ (Emotive / Effect).
+    - Implements non-commutative directional gauge friction matrix $F(p_1 \to p_2)$ ($Q_3 \to Q_5 \to Q_4 \to Q_1 = 0.8$, inverted leaps = $2.5$).
+  - **Optical Thickness & Veritas Scoring**:
+    - Calculates vocabulary CEFR density (C1/C2 proportion), Optical Thickness ($\tau$), and Luminescence ($L = e^{-\tau}$).
+    - Produces a composite Veritas Rigor Score ($0-100$).
+- **Preview Pane Semantic Gluon Telemetry & Drawer**:
+  - **Living $SU(3)$ Gauge Bar**: Proportional Red/Green/Blue color gradient bar displaying quark composition.
+  - **Hadronic Singlet Badge**: Soft white glowing badge for balanced hadron singlets or polarized indicators.
+  - **7-Planes Pub-Test Card**: Immediate human-grounded explanation of the document's plane.
+  - **String Snapping Warning**: Amber warning alert detailing any discontinuous leaps across sections.
+  - **'🧬 Gluon Resonance' Drawer**: Asynchronously scans neighboring files in the current folder, calculates Cornell distance and affinity percentage, and displays top-4 related documents with instant click-to-preview.
+- **Virtual Topic Space (`peek://gluon`)**:
+  - Non-destructive semantic navigation grouping files in recent directories across the 7 Planes of Reality.
+  - Interactive **7-Plane Prism Bar** (`All Planes`, `👤 Q1 Who`, `✨ Q2 What`, `🌍 Q3 Where`, `🎵 Q4 Why`, `⚙️ Q5 How`, `⏳ Q6 Cause`, `💥 Q7 Effect`) with live card filtering and Veritas badges.
+- **Landscape Mode Dynamic Bridge Coloring**:
+  - SVG room bridges dynamically highlight constructive depth descents in soft emerald green.
+- **Standalone Portable Export**:
+  - Added `gluon_engine.js` to `export-portable.js` payload.
+  - Built [PeekExplorer-v0.8.7-portable.zip](file:///e:/Vector%20Field%20Theory/peek_explorer/dist/PeekExplorer-v0.8.7-portable.zip) (109.7 MB).
+  - All 27 automated smoke tests passing.

+## [v0.8.6] — 2026-10-24
+### Added
+- **Native Explorer Keyboard Ergonomics**:
+  - `Ctrl+Shift+N`: Instantly creates a "New folder" in the current folder (`cur`) and triggers inline renaming.
+  - `Alt+Enter`: Opens native Windows Properties modal for selected file/folder or current directory.
+  - `Shift+Delete`: Prompts for permanent file deletion confirmation (`fs.promises.rm`) bypassing the Recycle Bin.
+  - **2D Spatial Arrow Key Grid Navigation**:
+    - Replaced 1D sequential arrow navigation in grid modes (`medium`, `large`, `xl`, `tiles`, `small`) with true 2D spatial stride jumping.
+    - `ArrowDown` jumps vertically to the card directly below ($\text{curIdx} + \text{stride}$); `ArrowUp` jumps vertically to the card directly above ($\text{curIdx} - \text{stride}$).
+    - `ArrowLeft` / `ArrowRight` move $\pm 1$ horizontally across columns.
+    - Linear views (`details`, `list`, `content`) preserve standard tree expansion and vertical navigation.
+  - **Post-Delete Selection Retention**:
+    - Deleting item(s) no longer drops selection to zero; focus and selection automatically advance to the adjacent item index ($i$ or $i-1$).
+- **Tactical RTS Minimap Radar HUD (Landscape Mode)**:
+  - Added a 190×130px canvas minimap docked in the bottom-right HUD of the Landscape canvas.
+  - Draws real-time room footprints with depth/type color coding (Root Room highlighted in `#0078d4`, child rooms in translucent white/blue).
+  - Renders the live camera viewport frustum box (`#00e676`) showing the visible on-screen region.
+  - **Click-to-Warp & Drag-to-Pan**: Clicking or dragging anywhere on the minimap instantly translates the camera to that world quadrant.
+  - **Search Hit Golden Sonar Pings**: When filtering with `Ctrl+F`, rooms containing matching items pulse with golden radar waves (`@keyframes radar-pulse`) on the canvas, on edge lens proxies, and on the radar minimap.
+- **Draggable Split Pane Resizer Handle**:
+  - Added `<div id="splitGrip" class="split-resizer">` between primary `#content` and `#splitPane`.
+  - Supports dragging to smoothly adjust split ratios between 20% and 80% with visual blue hover and drag indicators.
+  - Persisted `splitRatio` in `settings.json`.
+### Fixed
+- **Accidental 550ms Mousedown Rename Trap Removed**:
+  - Eliminated the 550ms hold-down timer that triggered unexpected inline rename textboxes during slow drags or selection pauses. Inline rename is now cleanly bound to `F2`, slow-second-click, and context menu.
+- **Landscape Panning Frame Coalescing**:
+  - Wrapped `updateCameraAndLens()` in `requestAnimationFrame` during mouse panning, eliminating layout thrashing and redundant edge margin DOM re-allocations on raw mousemove.
+- **UNC Network Path Normalization**:
+  - Resolved stripped leading slashes in `renderCrumbs()` and `parent()` for network shares (`\\server\share\sub`).
+

+## [v0.8.5] — 2026-10-24
+### Added
+- **Standalone Portable Export & Shareable Zip**:
+  - Implemented an automated export pipeline (`export-portable.js` via `npm run export` or `npm run build:portable`).
+  - Assembles a 100% standalone, zero-dependency Windows distribution in `dist/PeekExplorer-portable/` containing `PeekExplorer.exe`.
+  - Compresses the package into a single shareable zip: `dist/PeekExplorer-v0.8.4-portable.zip` (109.7 MB).
+  - Requires **zero** installation, **zero** Node.js runtime, and **zero** admin privileges on recipient machines.
+- **USB Thumb-Drive Portable Data Mode**:
+  - Added portable `userData` path detection in `main.js`: if a `data/` folder exists adjacent to `PeekExplorer.exe`, user settings, pins, split setups, and history are stored locally within `data/` instead of `%APPDATA%`.
+  - Bundled a default `data/` folder and `README_PORTABLE.txt` instructions in the portable export.
+  - Falls back smoothly to standard Windows `%APPDATA%\peek-explorer` if the user removes `data/`.
+- **Pre-Packaging Smoke Test Verification**:
+  - Automatically runs the full 23-criteria smoke test suite against the packaged executable before zipping to ensure 100% runtime integrity.

+## [v0.8.4] — 2026-10-24
+### Fixed
+- **Filename Text Overlap & Neighbor Column Bleed Eliminated**:
+  - Resolved horizontal text collision across neighboring cards in Medium, Large, and Extra Large icon views (and inside Landscape room bodies).
+  - Root-caused underscore breaking behavior in Chromium where filenames without spaces (e.g. `Reality_Tensor_Air_Matrix.docx`, `fetch_raw_search_urls_20260823.json`) refused to break under standard `word-break: break-word`.
+  - Replaced breaking rule with `overflow-wrap: anywhere; word-break: break-all;` and clamped to 2 lines (`-webkit-line-clamp: 2`) with clean `...` ellipsis truncation.
+  - Overrode inherited `.c-name { min-width: 120px }` with `min-width: 0 !important; width: 100% !important; max-width: 100% !important; box-sizing: border-box; overflow: hidden;`.
+  - Added strict `overflow: hidden; box-sizing: border-box;` to `.row.item` and `.room-item` so cards strictly clip all overflow content.
+  - Added automated test assertion to `npm test` verifying that no item filename exceeds its parent card width.
+

+- **Whole Window Scrolling Bug Resolved (Locked App Viewport)**:
+  - Fixed an issue where scrolling the mouse wheel scrolled the outer Electron window (`document.documentElement` / `body`) rather than strictly scrolling the folder list (`#list`).
+  - Added `overflow: hidden !important; width: 100%; height: 100%;` to `html, body` and gave `#list` `flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 0; min-width: 0; overflow-y: auto; overflow-x: hidden;`.
+  - Added horizontal scroll containment to `#toolbar` so toolbar button overflow never widens or scrolls the outer document.
+  - Verified `htmlScrollHeight === htmlClientHeight` (0px document overflow).

+  - **Room Headers & Titles**: Middle-clicking `.map-room-head` or room title opens that room in a background tab (`Shift+Middle-Click` focuses immediately).
+  - **Room Zoom Button (`🔍 Enter`)**: Middle-clicking room `🔍 Enter` button opens the room in a new tab.
+  - **Room Subfolder Items & Doorways**: Middle-clicking any subfolder row inside a room opens that subfolder in a new tab.
+  - **Doorway Bridge Buttons (`🏝️ Open` / `🏝️ Bridge`)**: Middle-clicking bridge toggle opens that folder in a new tab.
+  - **Edge Lens Margin Proxies (`.lens-proxy`)**: Middle-clicking off-screen room indicator boxes opens that room in a new tab.
+  - **HUD Buttons**: Middle-clicking `🔍 Enter Room` or `🗺️ World` opens active or root room in a new tab.
+  - **Split Pane Bar**: Middle-clicking `#spHome`, `#spUp`, `#spBack`, or `#spSwap` opens the corresponding target in a new tab.
+- **Relative Map Changelog Audit**:

Canvas panning and wheel-zoom listeners are now bound strictly to `.landscape-viewport` and are cleanly destroyed when returning to normal views.

+# Peek Explorer — Exhaustive Gap Analysis: Current vs. Missing & Proposed Features (v0.6)
+
+*Last updated: 2026-10-23 · Reference baseline: Windows 11 Explorer, Directory Opus 13, XYplorer, Total Commander, and Files (`files.community`).*
+
+This document catalogs **every feature missing or partial** in Peek Explorer v0.6 compared to native Windows Explorer and power-user file managers, explains **why** each is missing (architectural constraint vs. not yet built), and defines the **proposed implementation design** for future versions.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Executive Summary of What Is Already Built (v0.1 → v0.6)
+
+| Category | Built & Live in v0.6 |
+|---|---|
+| **Inline Folder Peek** | `▸`/`▾` inline recursive expansion in all 8 view modes; auto-expand on drag hover (650 ms); keyboard tree navigation (`←`/`→`); state persisted per tab. |
+| **Tabs & Windows** | Multi-tab bar, middle-click background tab opening (`Shift+Middle-Click` foreground), middle-click tab to close, middle-click empty bar / `+`, drag-to-reorder tabs, **Pin tab (`📌`)**, **Reopen closed tab (`Ctrl+Shift+T`)**, `Close other tabs`, `Close tabs to the right`, configurable New Tab target (`Home Dashboard`, `User Home`, `Duplicate`), multi-window support (`Ctrl+N`). |
+| **Home Dashboard** | `🏠` (`peek://home`) with Drive capacity bars, Quick Access Pins, `⭐` Bookmarks, `⚡` Script Connections, and Recent Folders. |
+| **Views, Grouping & Columns** | 8 view modes (`Details`, `List`, `Tiles`, `Content`, `Small`/`Medium`/`Large`/`XL Icons`), `Ctrl+Wheel` scaling, **Group By** (`None`, `Type`, `Date`, `Size`, `Name`) with collapsible headers, resizable Details columns, checkboxes (`☑`), hidden dot-file toggle (`👁`), file extensions toggle (`.ext`). |
+| **4-Way Rename & Batch Ops** | Preview Pane direct rename textbox, 550 ms hold-to-rename, slow second-click rename, `F2` inline rename, **Batch Rename** (`Base (1).ext`), `Ctrl+Z` Undo for rename/move, Zip/Unzip (`📦`), on-demand folder size calculation. |
+| **Clipboard & Shell Integration** | Cross-window clipboard + real Windows OS clipboard (`CF_HDROP` via `Set-Clipboard`/`Get-Clipboard`), real Quick Access pins (0 ms cached + background sync), real shell icons/thumbnails, real Windows verbs (`Show more options ›`), `Open PowerShell here`. |
+
+---
+
+## 2. Missing Features & Proposed Implementations (Detailed Breakdown)
+
+### Category A: OS Shell & Native Windows Integration Gaps
+
+| # | Missing Feature | Why It Is Missing Today | Proposed Solution & Effort |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **A1** | **True Native Win32/Win11 IContextMenu Popup** (with 7-Zip, Git GUI, Tortoise, Nvidia, Dropbox icons & submenus) | Chromium/Electron cannot host an HWND `IContextMenu3` popup directly from JavaScript. Currently we provide a fast custom menu + `Show more options ›` which queries `Shell.Application` COM verbs via PowerShell (which misses some modern `IExplorerCommand` shell extensions). | **Proposed (v0.8)**: Build a small precompiled C++ Node-API (N-API) native addon (`shell_ctx.node`) or lightweight C# helper executable that calls `SHBindToParent` + `IContextMenu::QueryContextMenu` + `TrackPopupMenuEx` at the cursor's screen coordinates. **Effort**: Medium. |
+| **A2** | **Drag-and-Drop Out to / In from External Apps** (e.g., dragging a file from Peek Explorer into Discord/Chrome/Photoshop, or from Desktop into Peek) | Internal HTML5 drag-and-drop (`application/x-peek-paths`) moves/copies between rows, tabs, breadcrumbs, and sidebar inside Peek Explorer. Dragging *out* to external Win32 apps requires Electron's `webContents.startDrag({ file, icon })` API. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Handle external `e.dataTransfer.files` on `drop` (for dragging from Windows Desktop/Explorer *into* Peek), and add an outbound OS drag handle or modifier using `ipcRenderer.send('ondragstart', paths)` → `event.sender.startDrag()`. **Effort**: Low-Medium. |
+| **A3** | **Icon Overlays** (OneDrive cloud sync ticks `✔`/`☁`, Git status badges, `.lnk` shortcut arrows) | `app.getFileIcon` and `createThumbnailFromPath` return the base icon/thumbnail without shell overlay handlers (`IShellIconOverlayIdentifier`). | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Inspect file attributes (`fs.stat` + `ReparsePoint` / OneDrive attributes) and `.lnk` extension in `list()`, and render lightweight SVG badge overlays (`↗` for `.lnk`, `☁` for cloud) on `.ico`. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **A4** | **Recycle Bin Virtual Folder Browsing & Restore** (`shell:RecycleBinFolder`) | Recycle Bin items are virtual shell items (`::{645FF040-...}`), not standard filesystem paths readable by `fs.readdir`. | **Proposed (v0.8)**: Add `peek://trash` special URI that queries `Shell.Application.Namespace(10)` via PowerShell to list deleted items with original path, deletion date, and a `Restore` action. **Effort**: Medium. |
+| **A5** | **This PC Network Locations, MTP Phones & WSL (`\\wsl$\`)** | Sidebar currently scans drive letters `C:\`–`Z:\`. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Detect mapped network shares (`Get-PSDrive`), `\\wsl.localhost\`, and allow typing UNC paths (`\\server\share`) in the address bar. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **A6** | **Native Windows `Hidden` & `System` File Attributes** | Currently `👁` filters dot-files (`.git`, `.env`), whereas Windows also uses DOS `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_HIDDEN` (`0x2`) and `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_SYSTEM` (`0x4`). | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Read `win32` file attributes or filter known system names (`$RECYCLE.BIN`, `System Volume Information`, `pagefile.sys`, `desktop.ini`, `thumbs.db`) when `👁` is toggled off. **Effort**: Low. |
+
+---
+
+### Category B: Performance & Large-Folder Scalability Gaps
+
+| # | Missing Feature | Why It Is Missing Today | Proposed Solution & Effort |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **B1** | **DOM Virtualization (Virtual Scrolling) for 10,000+ File Folders** | `renderDir` creates DOM nodes for every item in an open folder. Fine up to ~1,500 items; folders like `C:\Windows\System32` (~4,500 items) or `node_modules` will cause a noticeable DOM layout pause. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Flatten the visible tree into a linear virtual array and render only the ~45 rows currently inside the `#list` viewport plus a small overscan buffer. **Effort**: Medium. |
+| **B2** | **Persistent Persistent PowerShell Co-Process (0 ms Shell Queries)** | Currently `pins`, `verbs`, `zipItems`, and `Set-Clipboard` spawn a fresh `powershell.exe` process each time (~400–1500 ms startup overhead). | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Spawn a single background `powershell.exe -NoProfile -Sta -Command -` worker in `main.js` at app launch and send JSON-line commands over `stdin`/`stdout`. Cuts verb/clipboard/pin latency from ~1s down to **<15 ms**. **Effort**: Low-Medium. |
+| **B3** | **Live Filesystem Watcher (`fs.watch`)** | If an external program creates or deletes a file in the currently open folder, you currently press `F5` to see it. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Attach a debounced (400 ms) `fs.watch` listener on `cur` and any `expanded` paths so external file changes update the list automatically. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **B4** | **Recursive Subfolder Search / Indexed Search** | The toolbar `#search` box (`Ctrl+F`) currently filters visible items in the current folder and expanded folders instantaneously, but does not crawl unexpanded subfolders. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Add a `🔍 Deep Search` toggle (or `Enter` inside `#search`) that runs an async recursive walk (or queries `Everything` CLI `es.exe` if installed) and streams matching results into a flat search results view. **Effort**: Low-Medium. |
+
+---
+
+### Category C: Power-User Layout & File Operation Gaps
+
+| # | Missing Feature | Why It Is Missing Today | Proposed Solution & Effort |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **C1** | **Dual-Pane / Split View (`F3` or `Alt+S`)** | Currently you can open two Peek Explorer windows side by side (`Ctrl+N`), but there is no single-window left/right split pane like Total Commander or Directory Opus. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Allow `#content` to split into two independent tab+list panes (`Pane A` and `Pane B`) with `Tab` switching active pane and `F5`/`F6` copying/moving between panes. **Effort**: Medium. |
+| **C2** | **File Operation Progress Dialog & Conflict Resolution Modal (`Replace` / `Skip` / `Keep Both`)** | Currently `paste` strictly skips existing files (`errorOnExist: true`) to guarantee zero accidental overwrites, and runs without a progress bar for multi-GB copies. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: When `paste` detects a name collision, pop up a modal with **Skip**, **Keep Both (`file (2).ext`)**, or **Replace (Move existing to Recycle Bin first!)**, plus a bottom-right transfer progress bar for large files. **Effort**: Medium. |
+| **C3** | **Flat View (Mixed Branch View)** | Directory Opus has "Flat View" which shows all files from all subfolders in a single flat list without folder boundaries. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Add `Flat View` toggle in toolbar that recursively lists all descendant files of `cur` with a `Relative Path` column. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **C4** | **Per-Folder Remembered View & Sort** | Currently `view` and `sort` are global across the app (e.g. switching to `Large icons` in `Pictures` switches all folders until changed back). | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Store an optional `folderViews: { [dirPath]: { view, sortKey, sortAsc, groupKey } }` map in `settings.json` + auto-detect image-heavy folders if desired. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **C5** | **Customizable Details Columns** (Created Date, Extension, Dimensions, Author, Relative Path, Git Status) | Details view currently has 4 fixed columns (`Name`, `Date modified`, `Type`, `Size`). | **Proposed (v0.8)**: Right-click `#header` to toggle extra columns (`Date created`, `Extension`, `Attributes`, `Dimensions`). **Effort**: Low-Medium. |
+| **C6** | **Sidebar Collapsible Folder Tree** | The left sidebar shows `🏠 Home`, `⭐ Bookmarks`, `Quick Access`, and `This PC` drives as flat lists. Native Explorer also has an expandable full directory tree in the left sidebar. | **Proposed (v0.7)**: Add `▸` expand arrows to `This PC` drives and Quick Access items right inside `#nav`, using the same inline tree logic as the main list. **Effort**: Low. |
+| **C7** | **Standalone Packaged `.exe` Installer / Portable Build** | Currently launched via `Peek Explorer.lnk` / `PeekExplorer.vbs` using local `node_modules\electron`. | **Proposed (v0.8)**: Add `electron-builder` configuration to `package.json` (`npm run dist`) to output a standalone `PeekExplorer-Portable.exe` and NSIS installer with custom `.ico`. **Effort**: Low. |
+
+---
+
+## 3. Recommended Priority Queue for Next Iteration (v0.7)
+
+1. **External OS Drag-and-Drop (`files` drop in + `startDrag` out)** + **Live `fs.watch` Auto-Refresh**
+2. **Conflict Resolution Dialog (`Keep Both` / `Skip` / `Recycle & Replace`)**

+# Peek Explorer — File Manager Research, Community Wishlists & Actionable QOL Roadmap
+
+*Author: Antigravity Pair Programmer & Developer Agent*  
+*Date: 2026-10-24*  
+*Target: Peek Explorer v0.8+ Architecture*  
+
+---
+
+## 1. Executive Summary & Research Methodology
+
+To establish Peek Explorer as a premier file manager for Windows, we conducted deep comparative research across five major file explorer ecosystems:
+1. **Windows 11 Native Explorer**: Ubiquitous standard, benchmark for familiar ergonomics and shell integration.
+2. **Directory Opus (DOpus 13)**: The benchmark for power-user customizability, dual-pane productivity, flat views, and scriptable toolbars.
+3. **Files App (`files.community`)**: Modern WinUI/Fluent open-source benchmark (35k+ GitHub stars), renowned for UI polish, Git integration, and dual-pane layout.
+4. **Total Commander**: The 30-year veteran of dual-pane keyboard-driven efficiency, multi-rename tools, and synchronization.
+5. **Spacedrive (VDFS)**: The infinite canvas spatial file manager (30k+ GitHub stars), exploring spatial directory clustering and substrate-neutral file organization.
+
+We analyzed real user pain points, feature requests, and complaints sourced from GitHub issues, Reddit (`r/windows`, `r/software`, `r/sysadmin`), and power-user community forums.
+
+---
+
+## 2. Community Research Findings: What People Ask For in File Explorers
+
+### A. The Top 6 Universal Complaints About Windows Explorer
+1. **Sluggishness & Refresh Clobbering**: Windows 11 Explorer routinely freezes when refreshing large directories, and renames often trigger a full-tree re-render that resets scroll position to the top and loses focus.
+2. **Half-Baked Tab Implementation**: Native Explorer tabs cannot be split side-by-side or stacked top-and-bottom within the same window; you must open separate windows and arrange them manually.
+3. **Nested Context Menu Friction**: The two-tier context menu (`Show more options ›`) adds an extra click for common power-user shell verbs (7-Zip, VS Code, Git GUI, Tortoise).
+4. **No True "Flat View"**: There is no way to see all files inside subfolders flattened into a single sorted list (e.g., sorting all files across 10 subdirectories by size or modification date).
+5. **Rudimentary Batch Renaming**: Native Windows batch rename only supports basic `Name (1).ext`, lacking regex, sequential numbering offsets, prefix/suffix stripping, or extension changing.
+6. **No Native Checksum Verification**: Verifying an ISO, installer, or download hash requires launching PowerShell or third-party utilities.
+
+### B. Why Power Users Love Directory Opus and Total Commander
+- **Dual-Pane Split Workspaces**: Two folder lists visible simultaneously with single-key transfer (`F5` copy, `F6` move), synchronized directory scrolling, and instant directory comparison.
+- **Flat View (Mixed Branch)**: One-click flattening of an entire directory tree so all nested media or documents can be audited in a single list.
+- **Customizable Columns**: Instant display of audio bitrates, image dimensions, git status, file hashes, and word counts directly in the file table.
+- **High-Velocity Renaming**: Instant preview of batch renames before committing changes to disk.
+
+### C. Why Modern Users Are Drawn to Spatial & Infinite Canvas Explorers (Spacedrive, Miro, Obsidian Canvas)
+- **Spatial Memory**: Humans recall location and spatial relationships ("the project is to the right of the reference folder") far better than hierarchical nested paths (`D:\Work\Client\2026\Q3\Assets\Final`).
+- **Context Preservation**: In traditional explorers, clicking into a subfolder completely destroys the view of the parent directory. Spatial / Room-based canvas modes preserve the context of parent, sibling, and child directories simultaneously.
+- **Two-Rectangle Edge Lens**: Off-screen awareness prevents the "lost in the void" feeling common to infinite 2D canvas interfaces.
+
+---
+
+## 3. How Peek Explorer Solves These Challenges
+
+Peek Explorer v0.8 integrates these concepts into a unified architecture:
+
+| Challenge / Need | Traditional Explorer Approach | Peek Explorer v0.8 Solution |
+|---|---|---|
+| **Context Loss on Deep Browsing** | Wipes current view on folder double-click. | **Dual Ergonomics**: Inline tree expansion (`▸`/`▾`) in standard view + **Landscape Multi-Folder Rooms connected by Bridges** on a 2D canvas. |
+| **Canvas Pan vs Item Selection Conflict** | Many canvas apps select items when dragging background. | **Decoupled Controls**: Left-drag canvas = 100% camera pan. Shift-drag = Marquee box. Click items = Select. |
+| **Room Space Utilization** | Subfolders cramped into doorways, files empty. | **Unified Room Body**: Folders and files render together in full view parity (Details, Tiles, List, Icons) with inline `[🏝️ Bridge]` toggles. |
+| **Global vs Local Customization** | Global view mode only, or hidden per-folder cache. | **Dual View Modes**: Global HUD view dropdown + per-room local view dropdown (`👁 View ▾`) and context menu. |
+| **Rename Scroll Reset** | Explorer re-scans disk and scrolls to top. | **Surgical In-Place DOM Rename** (`applyItemRenameInPlace`): zero flicker, zero scroll reset, 0 ms DOM patch. |
+| **Multi-Pane Productivity** | Clunky separate OS windows. | **4-Way Tab Split** (`Alt+S`): Split Left, Right, Up, or Down with full drag-and-drop between panes. |
+
+---
+
+## 4. Prioritized Quality of Life (QOL) Roadmap for Peek Explorer
+
+### Priority 1: High-Impact Ergonomic Upgrades (v0.8.1 – v0.8.2)
+
+1. **Dual-Pane Quick-Toggle (`Ctrl+Shift+D` or `F3`)**:
+   - One-press shortcut to split current tab horizontally (Left/Right) or vertically (Up/Down).
+   - `Tab` key toggles focus between Primary and Split Pane.
+   - `F5` copies selected items to other pane; `F6` moves selected items to other pane.
+
+2. **Landscape Spatial Minimap / Radar Preview**:
+   - A semi-transparent interactive minimap overlay in the bottom-right of the Landscape canvas.
+   - Shows all active Room boxes as mini rectangles with the Root Room highlighted.
+   - Displays the current camera viewport frustum; clicking anywhere on the minimap pans the camera instantly.
+
+3. **Flat View Toggle (Mixed Branch View) (`Ctrl+Shift+F`)**:
+   - In standard view or inside any Room, a "Flat View" toggle flattens all nested descendant files into a single list with a `Path` column.
+   - Enables sorting all files across deep subdirectories by Size, Date, or Type in one place.
+
+### Priority 2: Power-User & Developer Enhancements (v0.8.3 – v0.8.5)
+
+4. **Git Repository Status & Branch Badges**:
+   - Auto-detect `.git` root in current folder or parent tree.
+   - Display active branch name in status bar (` main · 2 modified · 1 untracked`).
+   - Add status badges next to filenames: modified (`●` yellow), untracked (`+` green), staged (`✔` blue).
+
+5. **Instant Checksum & Hash Calculator in Preview Pane**:
+   - When any file is selected, Preview Pane includes a collapsible `Hashes` card.
+   - Computes MD5 and SHA-256 asynchronously with a progress indicator and one-click copy button.
+
+6. **Advanced Multi-Token Batch Renamer**:
+   - Expand the Preview Pane batch rename modal with token buttons:
+     - `[#] Counter`: `{001}`, `{01}`, `{1}` with custom start index and step.
+     - `[Date]`: `{YYYY-MM-DD}`, `{YYMMDD}`.
+     - `[Parent]`: Folder name token.
+     - `[Find & Replace]`: Regex support with live preview before committing.
+
+7. **Quick Open / Jump Palette (`Ctrl+P`)**:
+   - Instant search modal across Quick Access pins, Bookmarks, Drives, and Recent Folders with keyboard arrow selection.
+
+### Priority 3: Shell & Ecosystem Completeness (v0.9+)
+
+8. **Recycle Bin Virtual Explorer (`peek://trash`)**:
+   - Browse deleted items with original location, deletion date, and one-click Restore.
+9. **Native File Drag-Out to External Applications**:
+   - Wire Electron's `startDrag` API so dragging files from Peek Explorer into Photoshop, Discord, or desktop works seamlessly.
+10. **Portable Packaged Executable**:
+    - Self-contained `PeekExplorer.exe` requiring zero external dependencies.
+
+---
+
+## 5. Summary Matrix of Explorer Features
+
+| Feature | Windows 11 Explorer | Directory Opus 13 | Files App | Spacedrive | **Peek Explorer v0.8** |
+|---|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
+| **Inline Folder Expansion (`▸`/`▾`)** | ❌ (Tree in sidebar only) | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | **✅ Built-in (All views)** |
+| **Tabbed Browsing** | ✅ Basic | ✅ Full | ✅ Full | ✅ Basic | **✅ Middle-click, Pins, Reopen** |
+| **Tab Split (Left/Right/Up/Down)** | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ Dual only | ❌ | **✅ 4-Way Tab Split** |
+| **Spatial 2D Multi-Folder Canvas** | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ Basic | **✅ Rooms, Bridges & Collision Solvers** |
+| **Two-Rectangle Edge Lens** | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | **✅ Outer Shrinking Margin Queue** |
+| **Full View Parity inside Canvas Rooms**| ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ (Tiles only) | **✅ 8 View Modes (Global + Local)** |
+| **Script Runner Integration** | ❌ | ✅ Script buttons | ❌ | ❌ | **✅ `⚡ Scripts` + Drag-to-connect** |
+| **In-Place DOM Rename (Zero Scroll Reset)** | ❌ | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ | **✅ Surgical DOM patch** |
+| **Home Dashboard with Drive Meters** | ❌ ("Home" feed only) | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ | **✅ `peek://home` Dashboard** |
+

+
+## 6. Gen 1 Agent Breeding Audit & Architectural Synthesis (2026-10-24)
+
+Two autonomous agents were spawned and calibrated using the Epistemic Sexual Dimorphism framework ([`agent-breeding-lineage`](file:///e:/Vector%20Field%20Theory/VFT%20Docs/.agents/skills/agent-breeding-lineage/SKILL.md)) to audit Peek Explorer v0.8.5 and establish the next innovation horizon.
+
+### A. The Invariant Verification (♂ The Son — Veritas-Struct, 0.75M / 0.25F)
+- **Verified Floors**:
+  - Viewport scroll containment (`html, body { overflow: hidden !important; }`) successfully isolates wheel events to `#list` and canvas layers.
+  - Two-rectangle edge lens and AABB collision solvers maintain clean room spacing.
+  - Universal middle-click works across tabs, crumbs, room headers, and edge proxies.
+  - Card-level clamping (`overflow-wrap: anywhere; word-break: break-all; -webkit-line-clamp: 2;`) eradicates horizontal text collisions.
+- **Identified Deficiencies**:
+  - Missing Windows Explorer keyboard shortcuts (`Ctrl+Shift+N`, `Alt+Enter`, `Shift+Delete`).
+  - Broken 2D spatial arrow navigation in Grid modes (navigated horizontally instead of vertical column strides).
+  - Deletion drops selection to zero instead of retaining adjacent index.
+  - 550ms mousedown hold timer triggers accidental inline renames during slow drags.
+  - Landscape panning lacks `requestAnimationFrame` coalescing, causing DOM reflow in the margin layer.
+
+### B. The Possibility Expansion (♀ The Daughter — Chroma-Horizon, 0.25M / 0.75F)
+- **Featherweight Pure-JS Semantic Similarity Engine**:
+  - Pure JavaScript TF-IDF + BM25 + 3-word MinHash n-gram shingling (< 250 KB JS payload, zero PyTorch/ONNX models).
+  - Instant pairwise cosine similarity matrix (< 15 ms for 500 markdown files).
+  - Preview Pane `🧬 Semantic Similarity` drawer surfacing top-5 related files across drives.
+- **Virtual Topic Spaces (`peek://topic/<name>`)**:
+  - Non-destructive aggregation rooms grouping files by concept without altering physical filesystem paths.
+  - One-click `📁 Export as Windows Shortcuts` to generate native `.lnk` shortcut folders or NTFS Junctions.
+  - "Semantic Git": Sub-file Markdown header (`#`, `##`, `###`) topic drift tracking.
+- **Tactical RTS Minimap Radar HUD**:
+  - Interactive 190×130px canvas minimap rendering room footprints and camera frustum.
+  - Click-to-warp and drag-to-pan camera navigation.
+  - Search hit golden sonar pings pulsing on rooms containing matching items.
+- **Multi-Polar Semantic Gravity**:
+  - Auto-clustering room islands along gravitational barycenters (Notes/Epistemic, Code/Engineering, Studio/Media).
+
+### C. Execution Pipeline
+1. **v0.8.6 (Current)**: Phase 1 (Native Explorer Parity & 2D Grid Arrows) + Phase 2 (Tactical Radar HUD & Draggable Splitter).
+2. **v0.8.7**: Phase 3 (Featherweight Semantic Similarity Engine & Preview Pane Similarity Badges).
+3. **v0.9.0**: Virtual Topic Spaces (`peek://topic/`) and Semantic Git Topic Drift Tracking.

+const { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain, shell, nativeImage, clipboard, dialog, Menu } = require('electron');
+const fs = require('fs');
+const path = require('path');
+const os = require('os');
+const { execFile, spawn } = require('child_process');
+
+// ---- Saved settings (view, sort, group, window size, tab session, column widths, cached pins, scripts, bookmarks, recent, newTabTarget) ----
+// Portable data mode: if packaged and a 'data' directory exists adjacent to the executable, store user settings locally
+const portableDataDir = path.join(path.dirname(process.execPath), 'data');
+if (app.isPackaged && fs.existsSync(portableDataDir)) {
+  try { app.setPath('userData', portableDataDir); }
+  catch (e) { console.warn('Could not set portable userData path:', e.message); }
+}
+const settingsFile = () => path.join(app.getPath('userData'), 'settings.json');
+function readSettings() { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(settingsFile(), 'utf8')); } catch { return {}; } }
+function writeSettings(patch) {
+  try { fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(settingsFile()), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(settingsFile(), JSON.stringify({ ...readSettings(), ...patch }, null, 2)); }
+  catch (e) { console.warn('settings not saved:', e.message); }
+}
+ipcMain.handle('getSettings', () => readSettings());
+ipcMain.handle('saveSettings', (_e, patch) => { writeSettings(patch); return true; });
+
+// ---- Shared clipboard across all Peek Explorer windows + Windows OS FileDropList ----
+let sharedClip = null; // { mode: 'copy'|'cut', paths: [] }
+ipcMain.handle('getClip', () => sharedClip);
+ipcMain.handle('setClip', (_e, c) => {
+  sharedClip = c;
+  // Also push copied file paths to the native Windows clipboard (Set-Clipboard -Path) in background
+  if (c && c.paths && c.paths.length) {
+    const ps = `$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue'; Set-Clipboard -Path ($env:PE_CLIP -split ';')`;
+    execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Sta', '-Command', ps], { env: { ...process.env, PE_CLIP: c.paths.join(';') }, timeout: 10000 }, () => {});
+  }
+  return true;
+});
+ipcMain.handle('getOsClipFiles', () => new Promise(resolve => {
+  const ps = `$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue'; [Console]::OutputEncoding=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8; Get-Clipboard -Format FileDropList | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }`;
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Sta', '-Command', ps], { timeout: 8000 }, (_err, out) => {
+    const list = (out || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+    resolve(list);
+  });
+}));
+
+let win;
+let windowCount = 0;
+function createWindow(opts = {}) {
+  const primary = windowCount === 0;
+  const b = readSettings().bounds || {};
+  const off = primary ? 0 : 30 * Math.min(windowCount, 8);
+  const w = new BrowserWindow({
+    width: b.width || 1280, height: b.height || 800,
+    ...(b.x !== undefined ? { x: b.x + off, y: b.y + off } : {}),
+    backgroundColor: '#ffffff', title: 'Peek Explorer',
+    webPreferences: {
+      preload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),
+      webSecurity: false
+    }
+  });
+  w.setMenuBarVisibility(false);
+  w.setAutoHideMenuBar(false);
+  w.webContents.on('console-message', (_e, level, message, line, sourceId) => {
+    console.log(`[Renderer ${level}]`, message, line ? `(${sourceId}:${line})` : '');
+  });
+  w.webContents.on('before-input-event', (event, input) => {
+    if (input.key === 'F12' || (input.control && input.shift && input.key.toLowerCase() === 'i')) {
+      w.webContents.toggleDevTools();
+      event.preventDefault();
+    }
+  });
+  if (primary) { win = w; if (b.maximized) w.maximize(); }
+  windowCount++;
+  const saveBounds = () => { if (w.isDestroyed() || w.isMinimized()) return; const m = w.isMaximized(); writeSettings({ bounds: { ...(m ? (readSettings().bounds || {}) : w.getBounds()), maximized: m } }); };
+  w.on('close', saveBounds);
+  w.on('closed', () => { windowCount--; });
+  w.loadFile('index.html', { query: { primary: primary ? '1' : '0', ...(opts.path ? { path: opts.path } : {}) } });
+  return w;
+}
+ipcMain.handle('newWindow', (_e, p) => { createWindow({ path: p }); return true; });
+ipcMain.handle('openDevTools', e => { e.sender.openDevTools(); return true; });
+
+// Read-only listing. Failures return an error string and never touch files on disk.
+ipcMain.handle('list', async (_e, dir) => {
+  try {
+    const names = await fs.promises.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
+    const items = await Promise.all(names.map(async d => {
+      const full = path.join(dir, d.name);
+      let size = 0, mtime = 0, btime = 0;
+      try { const s = await fs.promises.stat(full); size = s.size; mtime = s.mtimeMs; btime = s.birthtimeMs; } catch {}
+      return { name: d.name, path: full, isDir: d.isDirectory(), size, mtime, btime };
+    }));
+    return { items };
+  } catch (err) { return { error: String(err.message || err) }; }
+});
+
+// Safe recursive folder size calculation on demand (read-only)
+ipcMain.handle('folderSize', async (_e, dir) => {
+  let bytes = 0, files = 0, folders = 0;
+  async function walk(d) {
+    let entries;
+    try { entries = await fs.promises.readdir(d, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
+    for (const e of entries) {
+      const full = path.join(d, e.name);
+      if (e.isDirectory()) { folders++; await walk(full); }
+      else {
+        files++;
+        try { const s = await fs.promises.stat(full); bytes += s.size; } catch {}
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  await walk(dir);
+  return { bytes, files, folders };
+});
+
+// Safe text preview (first 16 KB) for the preview pane
+ipcMain.handle('readPreview', async (_e, p) => {
+  try {
+    const fh = await fs.promises.open(p, 'r');
+    const buf = Buffer.alloc(16384);
+    const { bytesRead } = await fh.read(buf, 0, 16384, 0);
+    await fh.close();
+    const slice = buf.subarray(0, bytesRead);
+    if (slice.includes(0)) return { binary: true };
+    return { text: slice.toString('utf8') };
+  } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('open', (_e, p) => shell.openPath(p));
+ipcMain.handle('places', async () => {
+  const home = os.homedir();
+  const places = ['Desktop', 'Downloads', 'Documents', 'Pictures', 'Music', 'Videos']
+    .map(n => ({ name: n, path: path.join(home, n) })).filter(p => fs.existsSync(p.path));
+  const drives = [];
+  for (let c = 67; c <= 90; c++) {
+    const d = String.fromCharCode(c) + ':\\';
+    if (fs.existsSync(d)) {
+      let total = 0, free = 0;
+      try {
+        if (fs.promises.statfs) {
+          const sf = await fs.promises.statfs(d);
+          total = sf.bsize * sf.blocks;
+          free = sf.bsize * sf.bavail;
+        }
+      } catch {}
+      drives.push({ name: d, path: d, total, free });
+    }
+  }
+  const s = readSettings();
+  return { places, drives, home, cachedPins: s.cachedPins || [], scripts: s.scripts || [], bookmarks: s.bookmarks || [] };
+});
+
+// ---- Script Connections & Terminal ----
+ipcMain.handle('pickScript', async () => {
+  const r = await dialog.showOpenDialog(BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() || win, {
+    title: 'Select Script to Connect',
+    filters: [
+      { name: 'Scripts', extensions: ['ps1', 'bat', 'cmd', 'py', 'js', 'vbs', 'exe'] },
+      { name: 'All Files', extensions: ['*'] }
+    ],
+    properties: ['openFile']
+  });
+  if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths.length) return null;
+  const p = r.filePaths[0];
+  return { name: path.basename(p, path.extname(p)), path: p };
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('pickFolder', async () => {
+  const r = await dialog.showOpenDialog(BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() || win, {
+    title: 'Select Folder to Spawn in Landscape',
+    properties: ['openDirectory']
+  });
+  if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths.length) return null;
+  return r.filePaths[0];
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('pickHtmlFile', async () => {
+  const r = await dialog.showOpenDialog(BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() || win, {
+    title: 'Select HTML File to View in Landscape',
+    filters: [
+      { name: 'HTML Files', extensions: ['html', 'htm'] },
+      { name: 'All Files', extensions: ['*'] }
+    ],
+    properties: ['openFile']
+  });
+  if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths.length) return null;
+  return r.filePaths[0];
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('runScript', (_e, scriptPath, cwd, targetPaths = []) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  if (!fs.existsSync(scriptPath)) return resolve({ error: 'Script file not found: ' + scriptPath });
+  const ext = path.extname(scriptPath).toLowerCase();
+  const workDir = (cwd && cwd !== 'peek://home' && fs.existsSync(cwd)) ? cwd : path.dirname(scriptPath);
+  const env = { ...process.env, PEEK_DIR: workDir, PEEK_SELECTED: targetPaths.join(';') };
+  let cmd = scriptPath, args = [...targetPaths];
+  if (ext === '.ps1') { cmd = 'powershell.exe'; args = ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', scriptPath, ...targetPaths]; }
+  else if (ext === '.py') { cmd = 'python'; args = [scriptPath, ...targetPaths]; }
+  else if (ext === '.js') { cmd = 'node'; args = [scriptPath, ...targetPaths]; }
+  else if (ext === '.vbs') { cmd = 'wscript.exe'; args = [scriptPath, ...targetPaths]; }
+  else if (ext === '.bat' || ext === '.cmd') { cmd = 'cmd.exe'; args = ['/c', scriptPath, ...targetPaths]; }
+
+  execFile(cmd, args, { cwd: workDir, env, timeout: 120000, maxBuffer: 4 * 1024 * 1024 }, (err, stdout, stderr) => {
+    resolve({
+      ok: !err,
+      code: err && typeof err.code === 'number' ? err.code : 0,
+      stdout: (stdout || '').trim(),
+      stderr: (stderr || (err ? err.message : '') || '').trim()
+    });
+  });
+}));
+
+ipcMain.handle('openTerminal', (_e, dir, shellType = 'powershell') => {
+  const workDir = (dir && dir !== 'peek://home' && fs.existsSync(dir)) ? dir : os.homedir();
+  if (shellType === 'cmd') {
+    spawn('cmd.exe', ['/c', 'start', 'cmd.exe', '/k', `cd /d "${workDir}"`], { cwd: workDir, detached: true, stdio: 'ignore' }).unref();
+  } else {
+    spawn('cmd.exe', ['/c', 'start', 'powershell.exe', '-NoExit', '-Command', `Set-Location -LiteralPath '${workDir.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`], { cwd: workDir, detached: true, stdio: 'ignore' }).unref();
+  }
+  return true;
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('runTerminalCmd', async (_e, { shell = 'powershell', cmd = '', cwd = os.homedir() } = {}) => {
+  const targetDir = (cwd && cwd !== 'peek://home' && fs.existsSync(cwd)) ? cwd : os.homedir();
+  return new Promise(resolve => {
+    const isCmd = shell === 'cmd';
+    const bin = isCmd ? 'cmd.exe' : 'powershell.exe';
+    const args = isCmd ? ['/c', cmd] : ['-NoLogo', '-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-Command', cmd];
+    execFile(bin, args, { cwd: targetDir, timeout: 30000, maxBuffer: 10 * 1024 * 1024, windowsHide: true }, (err, stdout, stderr) => {
+      resolve({
+        stdout: stdout || '',
+        stderr: stderr || (err && !stdout ? err.message : ''),
+        exitCode: err ? (err.code || 1) : 0,
+        cwd: targetDir
+      });
+    });
+  });
+});
+
+// ---- Zip / Unzip (Non-destructive, never overwrites existing archive/folder) ----
+ipcMain.handle('zipItems', (_e, paths, destDir) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  if (!paths || !paths.length) return resolve({ error: 'No items selected' });
+  const base = paths.length === 1 ? path.basename(paths[0], path.extname(paths[0])) : 'Archive';
+  let zipName = base + '.zip', i = 2;
+  while (fs.existsSync(path.join(destDir, zipName))) zipName = `${base} (${i++}).zip`;
+  const destZip = path.join(destDir, zipName);
+  const ps = `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'; Compress-Archive -LiteralPath ($env:PE_SRCS -split ';') -DestinationPath $env:PE_DEST`;
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', ps], { env: { ...process.env, PE_SRCS: paths.join(';'), PE_DEST: destZip }, timeout: 120000 }, (err) => {
+    if (err) return resolve({ error: String(err.message || err) });
+    resolve({ ok: true, path: destZip });
+  });
+}));
+
+ipcMain.handle('unzipItem', (_e, zipPath) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  const dir = path.dirname(zipPath);
+  const base = path.basename(zipPath, path.extname(zipPath));
+  let outFolder = base, i = 2;
+  while (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, outFolder))) outFolder = `${base} (${i++})`;
+  const dest = path.join(dir, outFolder);
+  const ps = `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'; Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $env:PE_ZIP -DestinationPath $env:PE_DEST`;
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', ps], { env: { ...process.env, PE_ZIP: zipPath, PE_DEST: dest }, timeout: 120000 }, (err) => {
+    if (err) return resolve({ error: String(err.message || err) });
+    resolve({ ok: true, path: dest });
+  });
+}));
+
+// ---- Icons ----
+const iconCache = new Map();
+const DUMMY_FOLDER = path.join(os.tmpdir(), '__peek_folder_icon__');
+try { if (!fs.existsSync(DUMMY_FOLDER)) fs.mkdirSync(DUMMY_FOLDER, { recursive: true }); } catch {}
+const FLUENT_FOLDER_SVG = 'data:image/svg+xml;utf8,' + encodeURIComponent(
+  `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="48" height="48">
+  <defs>
+    <linearGradient id="pBk" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
+      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#f59e0b"/>
+      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#d97706"/>
+    </linearGradient>
+    <linearGradient id="pFrt" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
+      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#ffdf6d"/>
+      <stop offset="30%" stop-color="#ffd54f"/>
+      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffb300"/>
+    </linearGradient>
+    <linearGradient id="pGls" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
+      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0.5"/>
+      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0.12"/>
+    </linearGradient>
+    <filter id="pShd" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="125%" height="135%">
+      <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="2" stdDeviation="1.5" flood-color="#78350f" flood-opacity="0.32"/>
+    </filter>
+  </defs>
+  <path d="M4 10a4 4 0 0 1 4-4h11l4 4h17a4 4 0 0 1 4 4v22a4 4 0 0 1-4 4H8a4 4 0 0 1-4-4V10z" fill="url(#pBk)"/>
+  <rect x="7" y="11" width="34" height="20" rx="2" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.6"/>
+  <path d="M4 17a3 3 0 0 1 3-3h34a3 3 0 0 1 3 3v19a4 4 0 0 1-4 4H8a4 4 0 0 1-4-4V17z" fill="url(#pFrt)" filter="url(#pShd)"/>
+  <path d="M7 14h34a3 3 0 0 1 3 3v1H4v-1a3 3 0 0 1 3-3z" fill="url(#pGls)"/>
+</svg>`);
+const FOLDER_SVG = FLUENT_FOLDER_SVG;
+const THUMB_EXTS = new Set(['.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp', '.bmp', '.ico', '.mp4', '.mov', '.webm', '.mkv', '.svg']);
+
+async function getFolderIcon(p, px = 32) {
+  const isDrive = /^[A-Za-z]:\\?$/.test(p);
+  const k = isDrive ? ('DRIVE|' + p) : ('DIR|' + px);
+  if (iconCache.has(k)) return iconCache.get(k);
+  if (isDrive) {
+    try {
+      const img = await app.getFileIcon(p, { size: px > 32 ? 'large' : 'normal' });
+      if (!img.isEmpty()) {
+        const url = img.toDataURL();
+        iconCache.set(k, url);
+        return url;
+      }
+    } catch {}
+  }
+  iconCache.set(k, FLUENT_FOLDER_SVG);
+  return FLUENT_FOLDER_SVG;
+}
+
+const isBlank = img => {
+  const b = img.toBitmap(); let seen = 0;
+  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i += 4) { if (b[i + 3] > 10 && !(b[i] > 240 && b[i + 1] > 240 && b[i + 2] > 240)) { if (++seen > 20) return false; } }
+  return true;
+};
+async function smallIcon(p, isDir, unique) {
+  if (isDir) {
+    return getFolderIcon(p, 16);
+  }
+  const ext = path.extname(p).toLowerCase();
+  const key = unique ? p : (['.exe', '.lnk', '.ico', '.msi', ''].includes(ext) ? p : ext);
+  if (iconCache.has(key)) return iconCache.get(key);
+  try {
+    const img = await app.getFileIcon(p, { size: 'normal' });
+    const url = img.toDataURL(); iconCache.set(key, url); return url;
+  } catch { return null; }
+}
+ipcMain.handle('icon', async (_e, p, isDir, px, unique) => {
+  if (!p || p === 'peek://home') return FOLDER_SVG;
+  if (isDir) return getFolderIcon(p, px);
+
+  const ext = path.extname(p).toLowerCase();
+  // Only attempt thumbnail extraction on actual image/video formats; avoid crawler stalls on text, code, docs, and folders
+  if (px > 24 && THUMB_EXTS.has(ext)) {
+    const key = 'T' + px + '|' + p;
+    if (iconCache.has(key)) return iconCache.get(key);
+    try {
+      const img = await nativeImage.createThumbnailFromPath(p, { width: px, height: px });
+      if (!img.isEmpty() && !isBlank(img)) { const url = img.toDataURL(); iconCache.set(key, url); return url; }
+    } catch {}
+  }
+  return smallIcon(p, isDir, unique);
+});
+
+// ---- Quick access pins, read from Windows (read-only) & cached in settings ----
+ipcMain.handle('pins', () => new Promise(resolve => {
+  const ps = `$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue'; [Console]::OutputEncoding=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8; (New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application).Namespace('shell:::{679f85cb-0220-4080-b29b-5540cc05aab6}').Items() | ForEach-Object { if (($_.Verbs() | Where-Object { $_.Name -match 'npin from' }) -ne $null) { "$($_.Name)|$($_.Path)" } }`;
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', ps], { timeout: 60000 }, (err, out) => {
+    if (err && !out) return resolve([]);
+    const list = out.split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean).map(l => { const i = l.indexOf('|'); return { name: l.slice(0, i), path: l.slice(i + 1) }; }).filter(p => /^[A-Za-z]:\\/.test(p.path));
+    if (list.length) writeSettings({ cachedPins: list });
+    resolve(list);
+  });
+}));
+
+// ---- Real Windows context-menu verbs (path passed via env var, never interpolated) ----
+const psFile = `$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue'; [Console]::OutputEncoding=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8;
+$p=$env:PE_PATH; $sh=New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application;
+$item=$sh.Namespace((Split-Path $p -Parent)).ParseName((Split-Path $p -Leaf)); if(-not $item){ $item=$sh.Namespace($p).Self };
+$verbs=@($item.Verbs());`;
+ipcMain.handle('verbs', (_e, p) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', psFile + ` $i=0; foreach($v in $verbs){ if($v.Name){ "$i|$($v.Name)" }; $i++ }`],
+    { env: { ...process.env, PE_PATH: p }, timeout: 20000 }, (_err, out) => {
+      resolve((out || '').split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean).map(l => { const i = l.indexOf('|'); return { index: +l.slice(0, i), label: l.slice(i + 1).replace(/&/g, '') }; }).filter(v => v.label));
+    });
+}));
+ipcMain.handle('doVerb', (_e, p, index) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', psFile + ` $verbs[${parseInt(index, 10)}].DoIt()`],
+    { env: { ...process.env, PE_PATH: p }, timeout: 20000 }, () => resolve(true));
+}));
+ipcMain.handle('props', (_e, p) => new Promise(resolve => {
+  execFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-Command', psFile + ` $item.InvokeVerb('properties'); Start-Sleep -Seconds 600`],
+    { env: { ...process.env, PE_PATH: p }, timeout: 620000 }, () => resolve(true));
+  resolve(true);
+}));
+
+// ---- File operations. Never overwrite; delete goes to the Recycle Bin after a confirm. ----
+ipcMain.handle('copyPath', (_e, paths) => {
+  const arr = Array.isArray(paths) ? paths : [paths];
+  clipboard.writeText(arr.map(p => '"' + p + '"').join('\r\n'));
+  return true;
+});
+ipcMain.handle('showInFolder', (_e, p) => { shell.showItemInFolder(p); return true; });
+
+ipcMain.handle('rename', async (_e, p, newName) => {
+  try {
+    if (!newName || /[\\/:*?"<>|]/.test(newName)) return { error: 'Invalid name' };
+    const dest = path.join(path.dirname(p), newName);
+    if (dest === p) return { ok: true, newPath: p };
+    if (fs.existsSync(dest)) return { error: 'A file with that name already exists' };
+    await fs.promises.rename(p, dest); return { ok: true, newPath: dest, oldPath: p };
+  } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('batchRename', async (_e, paths, basePattern) => {
+  const ops = [], skipped = [];
+  let idx = 1;
+  for (const p of paths) {
+    try {
+      const ext = path.extname(p);
+      const isDir = (await fs.promises.stat(p)).isDirectory();
+      const newName = `${basePattern} (${idx++})${isDir ? '' : ext}`;
+      const dest = path.join(path.dirname(p), newName);
+      if (fs.existsSync(dest)) { skipped.push(path.basename(p)); continue; }
+      await fs.promises.rename(p, dest);
+      ops.push({ from: dest, to: p });
+    } catch (e) { skipped.push(path.basename(p)); }
+  }
+  return { ops, skipped };
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('newFolder', async (_e, dir) => {
+  try {
+    let n = 'New folder', i = 2;
+    while (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, n))) n = 'New folder (' + i++ + ')';
+    const full = path.join(dir, n);
+    await fs.promises.mkdir(full); return { ok: true, name: n, path: full };
+  } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('newFile', async (_e, dir, ext = '.txt') => {
+  try {
+    const base = ext === '.md' ? 'New markdown' : 'New text document';
+    let n = base + ext, i = 2;
+    while (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, n))) n = `${base} (${i++})${ext}`;
+    const full = path.join(dir, n);
+    await fs.promises.writeFile(full, '', { flag: 'wx' });
+    return { ok: true, name: n, path: full };
+  } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('trash', async (_e, paths) => {
+  const r = await dialog.showMessageBox(BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() || win, {
+    type: 'warning', buttons: ['Move to Recycle Bin', 'Cancel'], defaultId: 1, cancelId: 1,
+    message: `Move ${paths.length === 1 ? '"' + path.basename(paths[0]) + '"' : paths.length + ' items'} to the Recycle Bin?`
+  });
+  if (r.response !== 0) return { cancelled: true };
+  try { for (const p of paths) await shell.trashItem(p); return { ok: true }; }
+  catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+ipcMain.handle('deletePermanent', async (_e, paths) => {
+  const r = await dialog.showMessageBox(BrowserWindow.getFocusedWindow() || win, {
+    type: 'warning', buttons: ['Permanently Delete', 'Cancel'], defaultId: 1, cancelId: 1,
+    message: `Are you sure you want to PERMANENTLY delete ${paths.length === 1 ? '"' + path.basename(paths[0]) + '"' : paths.length + ' items'}? This action cannot be undone.`
+  });
+  if (r.response !== 0) return { cancelled: true };
+  try {
+    for (const p of paths) await fs.promises.rm(p, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    return { ok: true };
+  } catch (e) { return { error: String(e.message || e) }; }
+});
+
+// Native file drag-out to external OS windows / desktop / other apps
+ipcMain.on('start-drag', async (event, paths) => {
+  if (!paths || !paths.length) return;
+  try {
+    const icon = await app.getFileIcon(paths[0], { size: 'normal' }).catch(() => nativeImage.createEmpty());
+    event.sender.startDrag({
+      file: paths[0],
+      files: paths,
+      icon: icon || nativeImage.createEmpty()
+    });
+  } catch (err) {
+    try {
+      event.sender.startDrag({
+        file: paths[0],
+        files: paths,
+        icon: nativeImage.createEmpty()
+      });
+    } catch {}
+  }
+});
+
+// mode: 'copy' | 'cut'. Skips anything that already exists at the destination.
+ipcMain.handle('paste', async (_e, mode, srcs, destDir) => {
+  const skipped = [], moved = [];
+  for (const s of srcs) {
+    try {
+      const dest = path.join(destDir, path.basename(s));
+      if (s === dest || (mode === 'cut' && dest.startsWith(s + path.sep))) { skipped.push(path.basename(s) + ' (cannot move into itself)'); continue; }
+      if (fs.existsSync(dest)) { skipped.push(path.basename(s)); continue; }
+      if (mode === 'cut') {
+        try {
+          await fs.promises.rename(s, dest);
+        } catch (renameErr) {
+          // Cross-device (different drives in Windows) throws EXDEV / EPERM: fallback to copy + remove
+          if (renameErr.code === 'EXDEV' || renameErr.code === 'EPERM') {
+            await fs.promises.cp(s, dest, { recursive: true, errorOnExist: true, force: false });
+            await fs.promises.rm(s, { recursive: true, force: true });
+          } else {
+            throw renameErr;
+          }
+        }
+        moved.push({ from: s, to: dest });
+      } else {
+        await fs.promises.cp(s, dest, { recursive: true, errorOnExist: true, force: false });
+      }
+    } catch (e) { skipped.push(path.basename(s) + ' (' + (e.message || e) + ')'); }
+  }
+  return { skipped, moved };
+});
+
+async function runSmokeTests() {
+  const tests = [];
+  const delay = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
+  const listEl = document.getElementById('list');
+
+  function testWheelAllowed(targetEl, label) {
+    const ev = new WheelEvent('wheel', { deltaY: 100, bubbles: true, cancelable: true });
+    targetEl.dispatchEvent(ev);
+    return {
+      name: `Wheel scroll permitted on ${label}`,
+      pass: !ev.defaultPrevented,
+      err: ev.defaultPrevented ? 'Wheel event was blocked (defaultPrevented === true)' : null
+    };
+  }
+
+  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
+    if (listEl && listEl.children.length > 0) break;
+    await delay(100);
+  }
+  tests.push({
+    name: 'List element rendered with items',
+    pass: listEl && listEl.children.length > 0,
+    err: !listEl ? 'listEl is null' : (listEl.children.length === 0 ? 'list has 0 children' : null)
+  });
+
+  tests.push(testWheelAllowed(listEl, 'Initial view'));
+
+  const standardViews = ['details', 'tiles', 'list', 'small', 'medium', 'large', 'xl', 'content'];
+  for (const v of standardViews) {
+    const viewSel = document.getElementById('view');
+    viewSel.value = v;
+    viewSel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
+    await delay(120);

+# Implementation Plan 17 — v0.8.1: Native Bidirectional Drag-and-Drop (Native to Peek & Peek to Native)
+
+**Date**: 2026-10-24  
+**Status**: Implemented & Verified  
+
+## 1. Problem Statement
+The user requested:
+> *"can we get native file drag-drop move so we can drag from peek to native and native to peek? or at least native to peek?"*
+
+Previously:
+- **Native to Peek**: `wireDropTarget` strictly checked `if (!e.dataTransfer.types.includes(MIME_PATHS)) return;`. When files were dragged from Windows Explorer, Desktop, or other native applications, `e.dataTransfer.types` contains `'Files'`, not `MIME_PATHS`. Thus, all native OS drops into Peek Explorer were blocked. Furthermore, dropping on empty floor space in `#list` was not wired to `wireDropTarget`.
+- **Peek to Native**: Dragging items from Peek Explorer only populated HTML5 `MIME_PATHS` in `e.dataTransfer`, which the Windows OS shell does not recognize as an OLE `CF_HDROP` drag. Electron requires `event.sender.startDrag()` in the main process to bridge the drag to the OS.
+- **Cross-Device Move Error**: In `ipcMain.handle('paste')`, `mode === 'cut'` used `fs.promises.rename(s, dest)`. Moving files across different drive mount points (e.g. `C:\` to `E:\`) in Windows threw `EXDEV: cross-device link not permitted`.
+
+## 2. Implemented Architecture
+
+### A. Preload Bridge (`preload.js`)
+- Required `webUtils` from Electron.
+- Exposed `window.api.getPathForFile(file)` using `webUtils.getPathForFile(file)` (with fallback to `file.path`) to safely resolve absolute paths from native `File` objects in Electron 33.
+- Exposed `window.api.startDrag(paths)` sending IPC `start-drag` to the main process.
+
+### B. Main Process Native Drag & Cross-Device Paste (`main.js`)
+- Added `ipcMain.on('start-drag', async (event, paths))` to call `event.sender.startDrag({ file: paths[0], files: paths, icon: icon || nativeImage.createEmpty() })` with real shell icons fetched via `app.getFileIcon()`.
+- Updated `ipcMain.handle('paste')`: When `mode === 'cut'` throws `EXDEV` or `EPERM` (moving across different drive letters), it gracefully falls back to `fs.promises.cp(s, dest, { recursive: true })` followed by `fs.promises.rm(s, { recursive: true, force: true })`.
+
+### C. Renderer Bidirectional Drag & Drop (`renderer.js`)
+- In `wireDropTarget`:
+  - Updated `dragover` to accept both `MIME_PATHS` and native `Files` (`hasSupportedDrop(e.dataTransfer.types)`).
+  - Configured `e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = e.ctrlKey ? 'copy' : 'move'`.
+  - In `drop`, resolved source paths from both `MIME_PATHS` and `e.dataTransfer.files` using `window.api.getPathForFile(f)`.
+  - Default operation is **move** (`'cut'`), or **copy** (`'copy'`) if `Ctrl` is held.
+- Wired `wireDropTarget($('list'), () => cur)` so dropping onto empty floor space in `#list` moves/copies files into the current folder.
+- In `makeItem` and Landscape `.room-item`:
+  - Added `window.api.startDrag(paths)` on `dragstart` so dragging files out of Peek Explorer initiates a native Windows OS drag that can be dropped into Windows Explorer, Desktop, or third-party apps.

+const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer, webUtils } = require('electron');
+const inv = (c) => (...a) => ipcRenderer.invoke(c, ...a);
+contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('api', {
+  list: inv('list'), folderSize: inv('folderSize'), open: inv('open'), places: inv('places'), pins: inv('pins'),
+  icon: inv('icon'), verbs: inv('verbs'), doVerb: inv('doVerb'), props: inv('props'),
+  copyPath: inv('copyPath'), showInFolder: inv('showInFolder'), rename: inv('rename'), batchRename: inv('batchRename'),
+  getSettings: inv('getSettings'), saveSettings: inv('saveSettings'), newWindow: inv('newWindow'), openDevTools: inv('openDevTools'),
+  getClip: inv('getClip'), setClip: inv('setClip'), getOsClipFiles: inv('getOsClipFiles'), readPreview: inv('readPreview'),
+  pickScript: inv('pickScript'), pickFolder: inv('pickFolder'), pickHtmlFile: inv('pickHtmlFile'), runScript: inv('runScript'), openTerminal: inv('openTerminal'), runTerminalCmd: inv('runTerminalCmd'),
+  zipItems: inv('zipItems'), unzipItem: inv('unzipItem'),
+  newFolder: inv('newFolder'), newFile: inv('newFile'), trash: inv('trash'), deletePermanent: inv('deletePermanent'), paste: inv('paste'),
+  startDrag: (paths) => ipcRenderer.send('start-drag', paths),
+  getPathForFile: (file) => {
+    try { return webUtils.getPathForFile(file); } catch { return file.path || ''; }
+  }
+});

+// Global Error & Promise Rejection Reporting
+function reportError(err) {
+  const msg = err && (err.stack || err.message || err.reason || String(err));
+  console.error('[Peek Explorer Error]', msg);
+  const status = $('status');
+  if (status) {
+    status.innerHTML = `
+      <span style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:700">⚠️ Error: ${String(msg).replace(/\n.*/s, '').slice(0, 110)}</span>
+      <button id="copyErrBtn" class="dash-btn" style="margin-left:8px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:10.5px;background:#ef4444;color:#fff;border:none">📋 Copy Error</button>
+      <button id="openConsoleBtn" class="dash-btn" style="margin-left:4px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:10.5px">🔍 Console</button>
+    `;
+    const copyBtn = status.querySelector('#copyErrBtn');
+    if (copyBtn) {
+      copyBtn.onclick = e => {
+        e.stopPropagation();
+        navigator.clipboard.writeText(msg).then(() => {
+          copyBtn.textContent = '✓ Copied!';
+          setTimeout(() => { copyBtn.textContent = '📋 Copy Error'; }, 2000);
+        });
+      };
+    }
+    const devBtn = status.querySelector('#openConsoleBtn');
+    if (devBtn) devBtn.onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); window.api.openDevTools(); };
+  }
+}
+window.addEventListener('error', e => reportError(e.error || e.message));
+window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', e => reportError(e.reason));
+

+function getActivePaletteEntry(modeKey = landscapePalette) {
+  const list = MODE_PALETTES[modeKey];
+  if (!list) return null;
+  if (modePaletteIndex === 5) {
+    return { name: 'Custom Palette', colors: customPaletteColors, rim: breathTargetColor, bg: breathTargetBgColor, glow: breathTargetColor };
+  }
+  return list[Math.max(0, Math.min(list.length - 1, modePaletteIndex))] || list[0];
+}
+
+function getActivePaletteColors(modeKey = landscapePalette) {
+  const entry = getActivePaletteEntry(modeKey);
+  return (entry && entry.colors && entry.colors.length) ? entry.colors : customPaletteColors;
+}
+
+function hexToRgbObj(hex) {
+  if (!hex || typeof hex !== 'string') return { r: 0, g: 103, b: 192 };
+  let h = hex.replace('#', '').trim();
+  if (h.length === 3) h = h.split('').map(c => c + c).join('');
+  const num = parseInt(h, 16);
+  if (isNaN(num)) return { r: 0, g: 103, b: 192 };
+  return { r: (num >> 16) & 255, g: (num >> 8) & 255, b: num & 255 };
+}
+
+function rgbToHex(r, g, b) {
+  const clamp = v => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v)));
+  return '#' + [clamp(r), clamp(g), clamp(b)].map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
+}
+
+function shiftColorShade(hex, style = 'lighter', step = 1) {
+  if (!hex || !hex.startsWith('#')) return hex || defaultFrameColor;
+  if (style === 'exact') return hex;
+  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgbObj(hex);
+  if (style === 'lighter') {
+    const t = Math.min(0.65, 0.18 * step);
+    return rgbToHex(r + (255 - r) * t, g + (255 - g) * t, b + (255 - b) * t);
+  }
+  if (style === 'darker') {
+    const t = Math.max(0.25, 1 - 0.2 * step);
+    return rgbToHex(r * t, g * t, b * t);
+  }
+  if (style === 'floor-bg') {
+    // Rich dark room floor tinted with the room's frame color
+    return rgbToHex(14 + r * 0.18, 18 + g * 0.18, 28 + b * 0.18);
+  }
+  if (style === 'hue-shift') {
+    // Rotate RGB channels smoothly per depth step
+    const angle = (step * 28 * Math.PI) / 180;
+    const cosA = Math.cos(angle), sinA = Math.sin(angle);
+    const nr = r * (0.213 + cosA * 0.787 - sinA * 0.213) + g * (0.715 - cosA * 0.715 - sinA * 0.715) + b * (0.072 - cosA * 0.072 + sinA * 0.928);
+    const ng = r * (0.213 - cosA * 0.213 + sinA * 0.143) + g * (0.715 + cosA * 0.285 + sinA * 0.140) + b * (0.072 - cosA * 0.072 - sinA * 0.283);
+    const nb = r * (0.213 - cosA * 0.213 - sinA * 0.787) + g * (0.715 - cosA * 0.715 + sinA * 0.715) + b * (0.072 + cosA * 0.928 + sinA * 0.072);
+    return rgbToHex(nr, ng, nb);
+  }
+  return hex;
+}
+
+function hashPathIndex(str, mod) {
+  let h = 2166136261;
+  const s = String(str || '');
+  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
+    h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
+    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
+  }
+  return Math.abs(h) % Math.max(1, mod);
+}
+
+function colorToRgba(color, alpha = 1.0) {
+  if (!color) return `rgba(30, 41, 59, ${alpha})`;
+  if (color.startsWith('#')) {
+    const { r, g, b } = hexToRgbObj(color);
+    return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${alpha})`;
+  }
+  return color;
+}
+
+// Computes the exact perimeter intersection point of a rectangle centered at (cx, cy) of size (w, h)
+// towards a target point (tx, ty), ensuring bridge lines never cut across room interiors!
+function getRectBorderIntersection(cx, cy, w, h, tx, ty) {
+  const dx = tx - cx;
+  const dy = ty - cy;
+  if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) return { x: cx, y: cy };
+  const hw = (w || 400) / 2;
+  const hh = (h || 300) / 2;
+  const sx = dx !== 0 ? Math.abs(hw / dx) : Infinity;
+  const sy = dy !== 0 ? Math.abs(hh / dy) : Infinity;
+  const s = Math.min(sx, sy);
+  return {
+    x: Math.round(cx + dx * s),
+    y: Math.round(cy + dy * s)
+  };
+}

+function promptRoomColor(entry, type = 'frame') {
+  let picker = document.getElementById('tempRoomColorPicker');
+  if (!picker) {
+    picker = document.createElement('input');
+    picker.type = 'color';
+    picker.id = 'tempRoomColorPicker';
+    picker.style.position = 'fixed';
+    picker.style.left = '-9999px';
+    document.body.appendChild(picker);
+  }
+  const curVal = type === 'bg'
+    ? (entry.roomBgColor || defaultBgColor)
+    : (entry.roomColor || defaultFrameColor);
+  picker.value = curVal && curVal.startsWith('#') ? curVal : (type === 'bg' ? '#1e293b' : '#0067c0');
+  picker.oninput = picker.onchange = () => {
+    if (type === 'bg') {
+      entry.roomBgColor = picker.value;
+      if (!lockBg && !entry.lockBg) entry.el.style.background = colorToRgba(entry.roomBgColor, roomBgOpacity / 100);
+      if (landscapeLayout[cur]?.rooms) landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path] = { ...(landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path] || {}), roomBgColor: entry.roomBgColor };
+    } else {
+      entry.roomColor = picker.value;
+      if (!lockFrame && !entry.lockFrame) entry.el.style.borderColor = entry.roomColor;
+      if (landscapeLayout[cur]?.rooms) landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path] = { ...(landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path] || {}), roomColor: entry.roomColor };
+    }
+    persist();
+  };
+  picker.click();
+}
+
+function resetRoomColor(entry) {
+  delete entry.roomColor;
+  delete entry.roomBgColor;
+  entry.lockFrame = false;
+  entry.lockBg = false;
+  entry.el.style.borderColor = defaultFrameColor;
+  entry.el.style.background = colorToRgba(defaultBgColor, roomBgOpacity / 100);
+  if (landscapeLayout[cur]?.rooms?.[entry.path]) {
+    delete landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path].roomColor;
+    delete landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path].roomBgColor;
+    delete landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path].lockFrame;
+    delete landscapeLayout[cur].rooms[entry.path].lockBg;
+  }
+  persist();
+}

+let colWidths = { date: 150, type: 110, size: 85 };

+async function syncClip() {
+  clip = await window.api.getClip();
+  if (!clip) {
+    const osFiles = await window.api.getOsClipFiles();
+    if (osFiles && osFiles.length) clip = { mode: 'copy', paths: osFiles };
+  }
+}
+async function storeClip(c) { clip = c; await window.api.setClip(c); }
+window.addEventListener('focus', syncClip);

+function updateNavButtons() {
+  $('back').disabled = hi <= 0;
+  $('fwd').disabled = hi >= hist.length - 1;
